# [ROCm][gfx1151] ROCM_RDNA_SINGLESEQ_ATTN: HIP attention on RDNA

[Upstream PR](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61421) · state: open · merged: False

## Original PR description

## Summary

New opt-in attention backend `ROCM_RDNA_SINGLESEQ_ATTN`: two HIP kernels for gfx1151 (RDNA3.5) tuned for one sequence per step.

- **When:** `--attention-backend ROCM_RDNA_SINGLESEQ_ATTN` on gfx1151 with `--max-num-seqs 1`. It is never selected automatically, so default backend selection is unchanged; on other devices or with `max_num_seqs > 1` it is rejected at selection.
- **Cache:** `TRITON_ATTN`'s packed HND cache (LBHNC) and metadata; anything the kernels do not serve runs `TRITON_ATTN`'s forward.
- **CUDA graphs:** full graphs for uniform decode batches (`UNIFORM_BATCH`; `cudagraph_mode=FULL` falls back to `FULL_AND_PIECEWISE`). Prefill runs outside graphs.

| Kernel | Serves | Supports |
|---|---|---|
| `csrc/rocm/rdna_singleseq_decode_attn.cu` | decode and spec decode, M = 1..8 | WMMA, GQA-packed (each KV byte read once), split-KV with in-kernel merge, CUDA graphs |
| `csrc/rocm/rdna_singleseq_prefill_attn.cu` | prefill and chunked prefill, M ≥ 9, any cached prefix | FlashAttention-2 on WMMA, split-KV when tiles leave the GPU idle |

Both: fp16/bf16, D ∈ {64, 96, 128, 256, 512}, `Hq % Hkv == 0`, causal full or sliding window, pages a multiple of 16 (hybrid pages included), unquantized KV. Not supported (Triton runs them): sinks, alibi, softcap, non-causal, quantized KV, multimodal bidirectional prefix, and a strided query (in-place RoPE on the QKV projection in eager mode). KV connectors and adaptive verification are rejected at selection.

Every build is an entry of a `constexpr` table (`csrc/rocm/rdna_singleseq_{decode,prefill}_configs.h`, 672 + 430 entries over 33 configurations) and each kernel is a template on one entry. `cmake/rocm_rdna_singleseq_attn.cmake` generates one unit per (kernel, head dim, dtype), only when the build targets gfx1151. The `_rocm_C` ops are `rdna_singleseq_{decode,prefill}_{configs,attn}`.

## Build cost

The 1102 builds in 20 units, 32-thread host: **22.6 s wall / 208 s CPU at `-j30`**.

## Accuracy

GSM8K, 500 questions, 5-shot, greedy, seed 42 (strict / flexible). Ours at `max_num_seqs=1`, reference at 16.

| Model | Reference | Ours |
|---|---|---|
| Qwen3.5-9B | ROCM_ATTN 87.80 / 87.60 | 87.80 / 87.40 |
| Gemma-3-12B w4a16 | ROCM_ATTN 87.80 / 88.60 | 87.60 / 88.20 |
| Llama-3.1-8B AWQ | ROCM_ATTN 72.80 / 76.00 | 73.80 / 77.00 |
| Qwen2.5-7B AWQ | ROCM_ATTN 77.80 / 82.00 | 78.00 / 82.00 |
| Gemma-4-E4B AWQ | TRITON_ATTN 73.80 / 73.20 | 73.00 / 72.40 |

## Kernel performance (gfx1151)

Ratio = baseline time / ours (> 1: ours faster), geomean per shape (Hq/Hkv/D, `w` = sliding window). Baselines: `TRITON_ATTN`, `ROCM_SEGMENTED_ATTN` (no autotune), `ROCM_ATTN`. `%roof`: median of the roofline reached. `–`: the baseline does not support the shape.

## Tests

- `pytest tests/kernels/attention/test_rocm_rdna_singleseq_hip.py tests/v1/attention/test_rocm_attention_backends_selection.py`: 228 passed.
- GSM8K, Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct, all 1319 questions, `max_num_seqs=1`: 0.328 for both this backend and `TRITON_ATTN`.
- End to end with Llama-3.2-1B at `--max-num-seqs 1`: same greedy output as `TRITON_ATTN` in eager mode, compiled with CUDA graphs, `cudagraph_mode=FULL`, and ngram speculative decoding.
- `pre-commit` (ruff, mypy, clang-format, typos, …) on every changed file: passed.

Not a duplicate: no other PR adds RDNA3.5 HIP attention kernels or a single-sequence backend. AI assistance (Claude Code) was used; the submitter reviews every line.

👋 Hi! Thank you for contributing to the vLLM project.

💬 Join our developer Slack at https://slack.vllm.ai to discuss your PR in `#pr-reviews`, coordinate on features in `#feat-` channels, or join special interest groups in `#sig-` channels.

PRs do not trigger a full CI run by default. Reviewers with write access and configured trusted contributors can comment `/ci run` for upstream CI or `/amd-ci run` for AMD CI only whenever CI signals are needed.

Once the PR is approved or has the `ready` label, the PR author can also use the corresponding `/ci run`, `/ci retry`, and `/ci cancel` commands, or their `/amd-ci` variants. New commits do not start upstream CI automatically.

If you have any questions, please reach out to us on Slack at https://slack.vllm.ai.
